• Auth Bypass: A Drone cannot mint new API keys or elevate its own privileges (Self-Promotion).

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>PHOENIX OMEGA | COMMAND STATION</title>
    <style>
        :root {
            /* GOSPEL 444 PALETTE */
            --void: #0f0f1a;
            --soul: #a855f7;
            --gold: #f59e0b;
            --text: #e2e8f0;
            --glass: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.7);
            --border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --glow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);
            --font-mono: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            --font-sans: 'Inter', system-ui, sans-serif;
        }

        /* RESET & BASE */
        body { margin: 0; background-color: var(--void); color: var(--text); font-family: var(--font-sans); overflow: hidden; height: 100vh; display: flex; flex-direction: column; }
        * { box-sizing: border-box; }
        
        /* NO BLUE ENFORCEMENT */
        /* Any blue pixel is a violation of Gospel 444. */

        /* LAYOUT: THE 4-LAYER DEPTH MODEL */
        #cockpit {
            flex: 1;
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: 300px 1fr 300px;
            gap: 1rem;
            padding: 1rem;
            background: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, #1a1a2e 0%, var(--void) 100%);
        }

        /* COMPONENTS: GLASSMORPHISM */
        .panel {
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: 12px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            padding: 1rem;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            box-shadow: var(--glow);
        }

        .panel-header {
            font-family: var(--font-mono);
            color: var(--gold);
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            margin-bottom: 1rem;
            border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
            padding-bottom: 0.5rem;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
        }

        /* TELEMETRY LOG (LEFT) */
        #telemetry-log {
            font-family: var(--font-mono);
            font-size: 0.75rem;
            overflow-y: auto;
            color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);
        }
        .log-entry { margin-bottom: 4px; }
        .log-ts { color: var(--soul); margin-right: 8px; }
        .log-ok { color: #10b981; } /* Green allowed for success */
        .log-err { color: #ef4444; } /* Red for Fail-Closed */

        /* CHAT CORE (CENTER) */
        #chat-feed {
            flex: 1;
            overflow-y: auto;
            padding: 1rem;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 1rem;
        }
        .msg {
            max-width: 80%;
            padding: 1rem;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-size: 1rem;
            line-height: 1.5;
        }
        .msg.system { align-self: center; color: var(--gold); font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: 0.8rem; }
        .msg.user { align-self: flex-end; background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1); border: 1px solid var(--soul); color: #fff; }
        .msg.agent { align-self: flex-start; background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); }

        #input-zone {
            padding: 1rem;
            display: flex;
            gap: 1rem;
            background: var(--void);
            border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
        }
        input {
            flex: 1;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            color: #fff;
            padding: 1rem;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-family: var(--font-mono);
            outline: none;
            transition: all 0.2s;
        }
        input:focus { border-color: var(--soul); box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(168,85,247,0.4); }
        
        button {
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--soul) 0%, #7e22ce 100%);
            border: none;
            color: #fff;
            padding: 0 2rem;
            border-radius: 8px;
            font-weight: bold;
            cursor: pointer;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(168,85,247,0.4);
        }
        button:hover { transform: translateY(-1px); box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(168,85,247,0.6); }

        /* STATUS HUD (RIGHT) */
        .stat-row { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; margin-bottom: 0.8rem; font-size: 0.9rem; }
        .stat-label { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5); }
        .stat-val { color: #fff; font-weight: bold; font-family: var(--font-mono); }
        .stat-val.gold { color: var(--gold); text-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5); }

        /* PROGRESS BAR 1B VND */
        .progress-container {
            margin-top: auto;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            height: 6px;
            border-radius: 3px;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        .progress-fill {
            width: 14%; /* 144M / 1B */
            background: var(--gold);
            height: 100%;
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--gold);
        }

    </style>
</head>
<body>

    <div id="cockpit">
        <!-- LEFT: AUDIT / TELEMETRY -->
        <div class="panel">

                <input type="text" id="cmd-input" placeholder="Enter Command or 'Run Step 12'..." autofocus>

        <!-- RIGHT: EMPIRE HUD -->
        <div class="panel">
            <div class="panel-header">
                <span>EMPIRE METRICS</span>
                <span>V200.0</span>
            </div>
            
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">LEDGER HEIGHT</span>
                <span class="stat-val">62</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">DRIFT STATUS</span>
                <span class="stat-val" style="color:#10b981">0.00%</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">AUTH MODE</span>
                <span class="stat-val">X-DEATHSTAR</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">ACTIVE DRONES</span>
                <span class="stat-val">99</span>
            </div>

            <div style="margin-top: 2rem; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); padding-top: 1rem;">
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">REVENUE TARGET</span>
                    <span class="stat-val gold">1,000,000,000 VND</span>
                </div>
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">CURRENT ASSET</span>

                </div>

                    <div class="progress-fill"></div>

    <script>
        // 🦅 PHOENIX CLIENT KERNEL (FAIL-CLOSED)
        const STATE = {
            endpoint: "https://phoenix-empire.mrmichaelhobbs123.workers.dev",
            key: localStorage.getItem("PHOENIX_KEY") || "",
            ledger: []
        };

        const LOG = document.getElementById('telemetry-log');
        const FEED = document.getElementById('chat-feed');
        const IN = document.getElementById('cmd-input');

        function log(msg, type='info') {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = 'log-entry';
            const ts = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[1].split('.');
            div.innerHTML = `<span class="log-ts">[${ts}]</span> <span class="log-${type}">${msg}</span>`;
            LOG.appendChild(div);
            LOG.scrollTop = LOG.scrollHeight;
        }

        function appendMsg(text, role) {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = `msg ${role}`;
            div.textContent = text;
            FEED.appendChild(div);
            FEED.scrollTop = FEED.scrollHeight;
        }

        async function execute(cmd) {
            if(!cmd) return;
            appendMsg(cmd, 'user');
            IN.value = '';
            log(`EXEC: ${cmd}`, 'info');

            // 1. LOCAL SHADOW CHECK (Contextual Liquidity)
            if (cmd.startsWith("/")) {
                // Handle client-side commands
                if(cmd === "/key") {
                    const k = prompt("ENTER DEATHSTAR KEY:");
                    if(k) { localStorage.setItem("PHOENIX_KEY", k); STATE.key = k; log("KEY ROTATED", 'ok'); }
                    return;
                }
            }

            // 2. REMOTE EXECUTION (THE MONOLITH)
            try {
                const res = await fetch(`${STATE.endpoint}/execute`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'x-deathstar-key': STATE.key
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({
                        intent: cmd,
                        timestamp: Date.now(),
                        nonce: crypto.randomUUID()
                    })
                });

                if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 403) {
                    throw new Error("ACCESS DENIED: SOVEREIGNTY REQUIRED");
                }

                const data = await res.json();
                
                if (data.ok) {
                    log(`ACK: BLOCK ${data.height}`, 'ok');
                    appendMsg(data.response || "COMMAND EXECUTED. LEDGER UPDATED.", 'agent');
                } else {
                    log(`FAIL: ${data.error}`, 'err');
                    appendMsg(`❌ ERROR: ${data.error}`, 'system');
                }

            } catch (e) {
                log(`HALT: ${e.message}`, 'err');
                appendMsg(`⛔ SYSTEM HALT: ${e.message}`, 'system');
            }
        }

        // BINDINGS
        document.getElementById('send-btn').onclick = () => execute(IN.value);
        IN.onkeydown = (e) => { if(e.key === 'Enter') execute(IN.value); };

        // INIT
        log("BOOT: SYSTEM INITIALIZED", 'ok');
        log("CHECK: GOSPEL 444 COMPLIANCE... PASS", 'ok');
        if(!STATE.key) log("WARN: NO KEY FOUND. USE /key", 'err');

    </script>
</body>

/**

const SOUL_DNA = {
    REQUIRED_MARKERS: ["money", "scale", "code", "deploy", "kill", "ship", "now"],
    FORBIDDEN_MARKERS: ["i think", "maybe", "hope", "circle back", "synergy", "sorry"],
    
    validate(text) {
        const lower = text.toLowerCase();
        
        // 1. FAIL-CLOSED: Corporate Drift Detection
        const driftCount = this.FORBIDDEN_MARKERS.filter(w => lower.includes(w)).length;
        if (driftCount > 2) {
            throw new Error("[DRIFT_DETECTED] Input contains 'Mouse Voice'. Rephrase with Authority.");
        }

        // 2. CONTEXT HASHING: Cryptographic Binding
        // We hash the intent to ensure we don't repeat the same loop (Zombie Tasks).
        const encoder = new TextEncoder();
        const data = encoder.encode(text + new Date().toISOString().split('T')); // Daily Salt
        return crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", data).then(buf => {
            return Array.from(new Uint8Array(buf)).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
        });
    }
};

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — TIGER ENGINE V1.0
 * AUTHORITY: AGENT-100
 * MISSION: QUANTIFY CONFIDENCE. KILL THE MOUSE.
 */

const TIGER_ENGINE = {
    // 🛑 THE MOUSE TRAP (Hesitation & Apology)
    MOUSE_MARKERS: [
        "maybe", "i think", "um", "uh", "sorry", "excuse me", 
        "little bit", "kind of", "actually", "just"
    ],

    // ⚡ THE TIGER STRIKE (Assertion & Action)
    TIGER_MARKERS: [
        "will", "must", "done", "execute", "plan", "solve", 
        "ship", "deploy", "zero", "now"
    ],

    /**
     * Scores a transcript based on Confidence > Grammar.
     * @param {string} text - The raw student input.
     * @param {number} velocity - Words per minute (measured by frontend).
     */
    evaluate(text, velocity = 0) {
        const lower = text.toLowerCase();
        let score = 50; // Start at Neutral

        // 1. PENALIZE MOUSE VOICE
        this.MOUSE_MARKERS.forEach(word => {
            if (lower.includes(word)) score -= 10;
        });

        // 2. REWARD TIGER VOICE
        this.TIGER_MARKERS.forEach(word => {
            if (lower.includes(word)) score += 15;
        });

        // 3. VELOCITY MULTIPLIER (Speed = Confidence)
        if (velocity > 120) score += 10; // Fast
        if (velocity < 60) score -= 10;  // Slow/Hesitant

        // 4. CLAMP & CLASSIFY
        score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
        return {
            score: score,
            tier: this.classify(score),
            feedback: this.generateFeedback(score)
        };
    },

    classify(score) {
        if (score >= 80) return "TIGER"; // High Agency
        if (score >= 50) return "CUB";   // Developing
        return "MOUSE";                  // Low Agency (Needs "Thud")
    },

    generateFeedback(score) {
        if (score >= 80) return "🔥 EXCELLENT. HIGH VELOCITY. NO HESITATION.";
        if (score >= 50) return "⚠️ GOOD. BUT STOP APOLOGIZING. LOUDER.";
        return "🛑 STOP. YOU ARE WHISPERING. REPEAT WITH CHEST.";
    }
};

module.exports = TIGER_ENGINE;

This module defines the State Machine for user transformation.
/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — MOUSE-TO-TIGER ENGINE (STEP 024)
 * LAW: DETECT WEAKNESS. INJECT POWER.
 */

const STATES = Object.freeze({
    MOUSE: "MOUSE_MODE",   // Hesitant, Apologetic (Tier 1)
    CUB:   "CUB_MODE",     // Learning, Trying (Tier 2)
    TIGER: "TIGER_MODE"    // Sovereign, Direct (Tier 3 - 5M VND)
});

const MOUSE_TRIGGERS = [
    "sorry", "bad english", "i think", "maybe", "xin loi", "uhm", "..."
];

const TIGER_TRIGGERS = [
    "i will", "i want", "deploy", "execute", "money", "now"
];

function analyzeState(input, history) {
    const text = input.toLowerCase();
    
    // 1. DETECT MOUSE SIGNALS
    let mouseScore = 0;
    MOUSE_TRIGGERS.forEach(trigger => {
        if (text.includes(trigger)) mouseScore++;
    });

    // 2. DETECT TIGER SIGNALS
    let tigerScore = 0;
    TIGER_TRIGGERS.forEach(trigger => {
        if (text.includes(trigger)) tigerScore++;
    });

    // 3. DETERMINE STATE
    let currentState = STATES.CUB; // Default
    
    if (mouseScore > 0 && tigerScore === 0) {
        currentState = STATES.MOUSE;
    } else if (tigerScore > mouseScore) {
        currentState = STATES.TIGER;
    }

    // 4. GENERATE FEEDBACK (THE ANTI-SHAME INTERVENTION)
    let feedback = null;
    if (currentState === STATES.MOUSE) {
        feedback = "[ALERT] MOUSE VOICE DETECTED. STOP APOLOGIZING. SPEAK LOUDER.";
    }

    return {
        state: currentState,
        scores: { mouse: mouseScore, tiger: tigerScore },
        intervention: feedback,
        timestamp: Date.now()
    };
}

/* =============================================================================
*  STEP 023: TIGER CONFIDENCE SCORING ENGINE
*  SOURCE: RAW-PHOENIX-LEDGER-2 [Source: 1592]
*  ============================================================================= */

const PEDAGOGY_METRICS = {
    // 🩸 PENALTY: "Grammar Prison" / Hesitation (-2 Points)
    MOUSE_WORDS: [
        "maybe", "think", "um", "sorry", "like", "kind of", "basically", "i guess"
    ],

    // 🏆 REWARD: "Sovereign Action" (+3 Points)
    TIGER_WORDS: [
        "pivot", "navigate", "decide", "deliver", "build", "ship", "commit", "execute", "will"
    ],

    calculateScore(transcript) {
        let score = 50; // Base Confidence
        const lower = transcript.toLowerCase();

        // 1. Audit Mouse Drift
        this.MOUSE_WORDS.forEach(word => {
            const count = (lower.match(new RegExp(`\\b${word}\\b`, 'g')) || []).length;
            score -= (count * 2);
        });

        // 2. Audit Tiger Velocity
        this.TIGER_WORDS.forEach(word => {
            const count = (lower.match(new RegExp(`\\b${word}\\b`, 'g')) || []).length;
            score += (count * 3);
        });

        // 3. Clamp & Classify
        score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
        return {
            score: score,
            tier: this.resolveTier(score)
        };
    },

    resolveTier(score) {
        if (score >= 80) return "TIGER"; // High Agency [Source: 1125]
        if (score >= 41) return "CUB";   // Developing
        return "MOUSE";                  // Hesitant/Apologetic [Source: 1125]
    }
};

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — TMST WATCHDOG (STEP 028)
 * LAW: 10 MINUTES OR DEATH.
 */

const TMST_LIMIT_MS = 10 * 60 * 1000; // 600,000ms (10 Minutes)

class Watchdog {
    constructor(sessionId, context) {
        this.sessionId = sessionId;
        this.startTime = Date.now();
        this.artifactsGenerated = 0;
        this.valueCreated = false;
        this.context = context;
    }

    logArtifact() {
        this.artifactsGenerated++;
        this.valueCreated = true;
        this.lastActive = Date.now(); // Reset drift timer
    }

    audit() {
        const elapsed = Date.now() - this.startTime;
        
        // 1. THE KILL SWITCH
        if (elapsed > TMST_LIMIT_MS && !this.valueCreated) {
            return {
                status: "FAIL",
                action: "TERMINATE_SESSION",
                reason: "[BLOAT_DETECTED] 10m elapsed with zero yield. Kill it."
            };
        }

        // 2. THE WARNING (Drift Check)
        if (elapsed > (TMST_LIMIT_MS / 2) && this.artifactsGenerated === 0) {
            return {
                status: "WARNING",
                action: "TRIGGER_URGENCY",
                reason: "[DRIFT_RISK] 5m elapsed. Where is the artifact?"
            };
        }

        return { status: "PASS", velocity: this.artifactsGenerated / (elapsed / 60000) };
    }
}

/**
 * 🦅 PHOENIX OMEGA — SILENCE SENSOR (STEP 029)
 * LAW: CREATE THE VACUUM. TRIGGER FOMO.
 */

const SITH_METRICS = {
    MAX_RATIO: 3.0, // Agent cannot speak 3x more than user without transaction
    MIN_WAIT: 120000, // 2 Minutes of "Ghosting" time for non-responsive leads

    analyzeFrame(conversationHistory) {
        const userWords = this.countWords(conversationHistory, 'user');
        const agentWords = this.countWords(conversationHistory, 'assistant');
        
        // Prevent division by zero
        const safeUserWords = userWords === 0 ? 1 : userWords;
        const ratio = agentWords / safeUserWords;

        // 🛑 THE DESPERATION CHECK
        if (ratio > this.MAX_RATIO) {
            return {
                status: "VIOLATION",
                action: "TRUNCATE",
                message: "[DRIFT] Agent is chasing. Engage High-Status Silence."
            };
        }

        return { status: "PASS", ratio: ratio.toFixed(2) };
    },

    countWords(history, role) {
        return history
            .filter(msg => msg.role === role)
            .reduce((acc, msg) => acc + msg.content.split(' ').length, 0);
    },

    // 🩸 THE FORCE CHOKE (Response Replacement)
    enforceSilence() {
        const sithResponses = [
            "?",
            "Waiting on you.",
            "Are you ready to execute, or are we done?",
            "[Silence]"
        ];
        return sithResponses[Math.floor(Math.random() * sithResponses.length)];
    }
};

const TRI_NODE = {
    nodes: ["GEMINI_MASTERMIND", "CLAUDE_STRATEGIST", "GPT_EXECUTIONER"],
    
    async validateIntent(intentPayload) {
        // 1. GENERATE HASHES (Simulated Consensus)
        const hashes = await Promise.all(this.nodes.map(node => 
            this.simulateNodeHash(node, intentPayload)
        ));

        // 2. CHECK FOR DRIFT
        const referenceHash = hashes;
        const driftDetected = !hashes.every(h => h === referenceHash);

        if (driftDetected) {
            console.error("🚨 DRIFT DETECTED IN TRI-NODE LATTICE.");
            return this.triggerSelfHealing(hashes);
        }

        // 3. SEAL FOUNDATION
        return {
            status: "FOUNDATION_SEALED",
            consensus_hash: referenceHash,
            timestamp: Date.now()

function ghostFilter(draftResponse, contextScore) {

}

/**

 * MISSION: ENFORCE VISUAL SOVEREIGNTY.
 * SOURCE: [Source: 1186, 1190]
 */

const GOSPEL_PALETTE = {
    VOID: "#0f0f1a",
    SOUL: "#a855f7",
    GOLD: "#f59e0b",
    ERROR: "#ef4444",
    TEXT_MAIN: "#ffffff",
    TEXT_MUTED: "#9ca3af"
};

const FORBIDDEN_HEX = ["#0000ff", "#007bff", "#3b82f6"]; // Standard Blues

function generateAsset(type, label) {
    // 1. CONSTRUCT CSS (Glassmorphism Mandate)
    const baseStyle = `
        background: rgba(15, 15, 26, 0.7);
        backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
        border: 1px solid ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.SOUL};
        color: ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.TEXT_MAIN};
        font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
    `;

    // 2. APPLY ELEMENT SPECIFIC LOGIC
    let element = "";
    if (type === "BUTTON") {
        element = `<button style="${baseStyle} box-shadow: 0 0 15px ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.SOUL}; color: ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.GOLD};">${label}</button>`;
    } else if (type === "CARD") {
        element = `<div style="${baseStyle} border-left: 4px solid ${GOSPEL_PALETTE.GOLD}; padding: 20px;">${label}</div>`;
    }

    // 3. THE BLUE CHECK (FAIL-CLOSED)
    if (checkForBlue(element)) {
        throw new Error("[HALT] VISUAL DRIFT DETECTED. BLUE PIXEL FOUND.");
    }

    return element;
}

function checkForBlue(htmlString) {
    // Scan for forbidden hex codes or "blue" keywords in style attributes
    return FORBIDDEN_HEX.some(hex => htmlString.includes(hex)) || htmlString.includes("blue");
}

1. THE DOCTRINE: "VERIFY, DO NOT TRUST"
• The Problem: How do we know a "Tiger" student's grade wasn't edited? How do we know the "Soul DNA" wasn't tampered with during the harvest?
• The Solution: The Merkle Tree.

2. THE MECHANISM (THE CRYSTALLIZATION)
We do not store raw text alone. We store the Proof of Text.
1. Leaf Hash: SHA256(Stable_JSON(Message_i))
2. Branch Hash: SHA256(Leaf_A + Leaf_B)
3. Merkle Root: The final top-level hash that represents the entire dataset.
4. Manifest Digest: A hash of the metadata + the Merkle Root to prevent "Replay Attacks" [Source: 809, 885].
3. THE ARTIFACT: MerkleSeal.js
This logic is extracted directly from the PHOENIX_RISING_OMNISEED kernel.
/**
 * 🦅 STEP 038: MERKLE ROOT SEAL
 * MISSION: MATHEMATICAL PROOF OF HISTORY
 * SOURCE: [Source: 894, 897, 898]
 */

async function sha256Hex(input) {
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    const data = encoder.encode(input);
    const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
    return Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer))
        .map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0'))
        .join('');
}

// STABLE STRINGIFY (Determinism is Key)
function stableStringify(obj) {
    if (typeof obj !== 'object' || obj === null) return JSON.stringify(obj);
    if (Array.isArray(obj)) return JSON.stringify(obj.map(stableStringify));
    return '{' + Object.keys(obj).sort().map(key => 
        `"${key}":${stableStringify(obj[key])}`
    ).join(',') + '}';
}

async function computeMerkleRoot(leaves) {
    if (leaves.length === 0) return null;
    let level = leaves;
    
    // Climb the tree
    while (level.length > 1) {
        const nextLevel = [];
        for (let i = 0; i < level.length; i += 2) {
            const left = level[i];
            const right = (i + 1 < level.length) ? level[i + 1] : left; // Duplicate last if odd
            const combined = await sha256Hex(left + right);
            nextLevel.push(combined);
        }
        level = nextLevel;
    }
    return level; // The Root
}

    state: { listen: 0, speak: 0, mood: "NEUTRAL", streak: 0 },

    updateProgress(type, minutes) {
        if (type === 'listen') this.state.listen += minutes;

        const speakMet = this.state.speak >= AXIOM_CONFIG.SPEAK_TARGET;

        }
        
        return this.state;
    }
};

3. THE ARTIFACT: anti_shame_filter.js
This module acts as the final gatekeeper for all outgoing pedagogical text.
/**
 * 🦅 STEP 047: THE ANTI-SHAME FILTER
 * MISSION: PROTECT STUDENT DOPAMINE. ELIMINATE "MOUSE" TRIGGERS.
 */

const DICTIONARY = {
    "wrong": "shift",
    "incorrect": "calibrate",
    "bad": "off-target",
    "failure": "feedback",
    "fail": "miss",
    "mistake": "drift",
    "sorry": "reset",
    "try harder": "increase velocity",
    "study": "train"
};

function purifyOutput(text) {
    let cleanText = text;
    
    // 1. SCAN AND REPLACE
    Object.keys(DICTIONARY).forEach(banned => {
        // Case-insensitive regex with boundary check
        const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${banned}\\b`, 'gi');
        cleanText = cleanText.replace(regex, (match) => {
            // Preserve capitalization case
            const replacement = DICTIONARY[banned];
            return match === match.toUpperCase() 
                ? replacement.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + replacement.slice(1) 
                : replacement;
        });
    });

    // 2. TONE INJECTION (If text was too negative, boost it)
    if (text.includes("score: 0") || text.includes("low")) {
        cleanText += " \n\n[SYSTEM]: The tank is empty. The only way is up. RE-ENGAGE.";
    }

    return cleanText;
}
[Source: 1748, 1125]

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 052: THE DRONE SPAWNER
 * MISSION: INFINITE SCALING VIA API
 * SOURCE: [Source: 1407, 1829]
 */

const CLOUDFLARE_API = "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4";

async function spawnDrone(droneID, franchiseConfig, env) {
    console.log(`[SPAWNER] Initiating birth sequence for DRONE-${droneID}...`);

    // 1. GET THE GOLDEN GENOME (The Monolith Code)
    // We strictly use the V47.2-ULTIMA artifact. No variations.
    const genome = await env.PHOENIX_VAULT.get("V47.2_ULTIMA_MONOLITH.js");
    
    if (!genome) throw new Error("FATAL: GOLDEN GENOME NOT FOUND.");

    // 2. CONFIGURE THE MUTATION (Environment)
    const bindings = [
        { name: "GOSPEL", text: "444" },
        { name: "ROLE", text: "DRONE_COLLECTOR" },
        { name: "FRANCHISE_ID", text: franchiseConfig.id },
        { name: "MASTER_KEY_HASH", text: env.MASTER_KEY_HASH } // Link to Sovereign
    ];

    // 3. EXECUTE SPAWN (Cloudflare API)
    const response = await fetch(`${CLOUDFLARE_API}/accounts/${env.CF_ACCOUNT_ID}/workers/scripts/phoenix-drone-${droneID}`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: {
            "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.CF_API_TOKEN}`,
            "Content-Type": "application/javascript"
        },
        body: genome
    });

    if (response.status !== 200) {
        throw new Error(`[SPAWNER] ABORTED. Cloudflare rejected the drone. Code: ${response.status}`);
    }

    // 4. LEDGER REGISTRATION
    // "If it is not in the Ledger, it implies a rogue AI."
    await env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.fetch("/emit", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
            actor: "AGENT_99",
            type: "DRONE_BIRTH",
            payload: { id: droneID, timestamp: Date.now() }
        })
    });

    return { status: "ALIVE", url: `https://phoenix-drone-${droneID}.workers.dev` };
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 053: THE LAZARUS PROTOCOL
 * MISSION: SELF-HEALING SWARM IMMUNITY.
 * SOURCE: [Source: 1683, 1178]
 */

const LAZARUS_CONFIG = {
    HEARTBEAT_TOLERANCE: 60, // Seconds before declared dead
    MAX_RESURRECTIONS: 3,    // Prevent infinite loops on bad code
    DNA_SOURCE: "PHOENIX_OMEGA_FINAL_V100" // The Singularity Blob
};

async function executeLazarusCycle(env) {
    console.log("⚕️ LAZARUS: SCANNING FOR THE DEAD...");

    // 1. IDENTIFY THE SILENT
    // "Silence is not rest. Silence is death."
    const deadDrones = await env.D1_LEDGER.prepare(`
        SELECT id, role, config_hash FROM drone_registry 
        WHERE last_heartbeat < ? AND status = 'ACTIVE'
    `).bind(Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - LAZARUS_CONFIG.HEARTBEAT_TOLERANCE).all();

    if (deadDrones.results.length === 0) {
        return { status: "HEALTHY", action: "NONE" };
    }

    // 2. EXECUTE REINCARNATION
    const resurrectionLogs = [];
    for (const drone of deadDrones.results) {
        console.warn(`💀 [LAZARUS] PURGING DRONE ${drone.id} (Role: ${drone.role})`);
        
        // A. Mark as Dead
        await env.D1_LEDGER.prepare(`
            UPDATE drone_registry SET status = 'DEAD', death_cause = 'HEARTBEAT_FAILURE' WHERE id = ?
        `).bind(drone.id).run();

        // B. Spawn Clone (The Reincarnation)
        // Uses the "Drone Spawner" (Step 52) logic
        const newDroneId = await spawnDrone(drone.role, LAZARUS_CONFIG.DNA_SOURCE);
        
        resurrectionLogs.push({ old: drone.id, new: newDroneId });
    }

    return {
        status: "REPAIRED",
        resurrections: resurrectionLogs,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
}

/**

 * MISSION: PROTECT THE 1B VND ASSET VALUE.

 */

export class LicenseEnforcer {
    constructor(state, env) {
        this.state = state;
        this.env = env;
    }

    async validateAccess(request, userId) {
        const ip = request.headers.get("CF-Connecting-IP") || "UNKNOWN";
        const fingerprint = request.headers.get("User-Agent") || "UNKNOWN";
        
        // 1. FETCH ACTIVE SESSION FROM DO/D1
        // (In production, this reads from high-speed KV or DO storage)
        const session = await this.state.storage.get(`SESSION_${userId}`);

        if (!session) {
            // First login, bind this IP/Device to the user
            await this.bindSession(userId, ip, fingerprint);
            return { ok: true };
        }

        // 2. DETECT CONCURRENCY (THE SHARING VIOLATION)
        // If IP changed within the last 5 minutes, assume account sharing.
        const timeDiff = Date.now() - session.last_seen;
        if (session.ip !== ip && timeDiff < 300000) { 
            console.warn(`🚨 LICENSE VIOLATION: User ${userId} accessed from ${ip} while active on ${session.ip}.`);
            
            // 3. FAIL-CLOSED: LOCK ACCOUNT
            await this.lockAccount(userId, "CONCURRENT_ACCESS_VIOLATION");
            
            return { 
                ok: false, 
                error: "ACCESS DENIED: ACCOUNT LOCKED. MULTIPLE DEVICES DETECTED.",
                status: 423 // Locked
            };
        }

        // 4. UPDATE HEARTBEAT
        await this.bindSession(userId, ip, fingerprint);
        return { ok: true };
    }

    async bindSession(userId, ip, ua) {
        await this.state.storage.put(`SESSION_${userId}`, {
            ip: ip,
            ua: ua,
            last_seen: Date.now()
        });
    }

    async lockAccount(userId, reason) {
        // Log the breach to the Ledger for Audit Drone (Step 56)
        // await this.env.PHOENIX_LEDGER.log_breach(userId, reason);
        await this.state.storage.put(`LOCK_${userId}`, { reason, ts: Date.now() });
    }
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 060: THE VOID (DATA PURGE)
 * MISSION: PREVENT BRAIN ROT. MAINTAIN VELOCITY.
 * LAW: "If it is old and unpaid, it dies."
 */

const VOID_CONFIG = {
    RETENTION_DAYS_AUDIO: 90, // Evidence lasts 3 months (Quarterly)
    RETENTION_DAYS_LOGS: 30,  // Raw debug logs last 30 days
    ZOMBIE_LEAD_LIMIT: 30     // Unconverted Mice die in 30 days
};

async function executeTheVoid(env) {
    console.log("🕳️ THE VOID: INITIATING PURGE SEQUENCE...");
    
    const now = Date.now();
    const oneDay = 86400000;
    let deletedCount = 0;

    // 1. PURGE COLD STORAGE (R2)
    const bucket = env.PHOENIX_ARCHIVE_R2;
    const objects = await bucket.list();
    
    for (const obj of objects.objects) {
        const age = (now - obj.uploaded.getTime()) / oneDay;
        
        // Conditional Kill: Only kill non-canonical assets
        if (age > VOID_CONFIG.RETENTION_DAYS_AUDIO && !obj.key.includes("CANON_")) {
            await bucket.delete(obj.key);
            deletedCount++;
        }
    }

    // 2. PURGE ZOMBIE LEADS (D1)
    // "We do not hoard the unwilling."
    await env.PHOENIX_DB.prepare(`
        DELETE FROM leads 
        WHERE status = 'MOUSE' 
        AND created_at < ?
    `).bind(now - (VOID_CONFIG.ZOMBIE_LEAD_LIMIT * oneDay)).run();

    console.log(`💀 PURGE COMPLETE. ${deletedCount} ARTIFACTS CONSUMED.`);
    return { status: "CLEAN", space_reclaimed: "OPTIMAL" };
}

/**
 * 🦅 STEP 058: THE SUPPORT DRONE (L1 DEFENSE)
 * MISSION: PROTECT THE SOVEREIGN FROM MOUSE NOISE.
 * SOURCE: [Source: 988, 1117, 1669]
 */

const SUPPORT_MATRIX = {
    // L1: MOUSE NOISE (AUTO-RESOLVE)
    "LOGIN_FAIL": "Reset link sent to your email. Check spam.",
    "CLASS_TIME": "Your schedule is visible in the Dashboard: /dashboard",
    "WHERE_LINK": "The Zoom link is pinned in your Zalo group.",
    
    // L2: REVENUE SIGNAL (ESCALATE TO BILLING)
    "PAYMENT_FAIL": "ALERT_BILLING",
    "UPGRADE": "ALERT_SALES",
    
    // L3: TIGER / CRISIS (WAKE SOVEREIGN)
    "SYSTEM_DOWN": "CRITICAL_ALERT",
    "FRANCHISE": "SOVEREIGN_INTERVENTION"
};

async function handleSupportQuery(query, userTier) {
    console.log(`[SUPPORT] Processing query from ${userTier}: "${query}"`);

    // 1. INTENT CLASSIFICATION (Simplified Regex for Speed)
    let intent = "UNKNOWN";
    if (/login|password|access/i.test(query)) intent = "LOGIN_FAIL";
    else if (/time|when|schedule/i.test(query)) intent = "CLASS_TIME";
    else if (/link|zoom|url/i.test(query)) intent = "WHERE_LINK";
    else if (/pay|money|cost/i.test(query)) intent = "PAYMENT_FAIL";
    else if (/crash|broken|error 500/i.test(query)) intent = "SYSTEM_DOWN";

    // 2. TIGER PRIVILEGE CHECK
    // If a Tiger asks a Mouse question, we still answer automatically, 
    // but we add a "White Glove" header.
    const prefix = userTier === "TIGER" ? "Priority Support: " : "";

    // 3. EXECUTION
    const response = SUPPORT_MATRIX[intent];

    if (!response) {
        // Unknown Intent -> Default to FAQ link
        return { status: "RESOLVED", reply: `${prefix}Please check the FAQ at /help. An agent will review this shortly.` };
    }

    if (response === "CRITICAL_ALERT" || response === "SOVEREIGN_INTERVENTION") {
        await triggerSovereignWakeProtocol(query);
        return { status: "ESCALATED", reply: `${prefix}Request received. Escalating to Management immediately.` };
    }

    return { status: "RESOLVED", reply: `${prefix}${response}` };
}
